Although Safety Nets have been studied, no comprehensive study of their performance characteristics and system design trade-offs has been conducted. This work presents the first systematic analysis of the trade-off between neural network and lookup table size in Safety Nets. By systematically comparing neural networks with diverse architectures, this study identifies optimal design parameters that minimize overall storage and memory requirements while maintaining certification compliance. Results demonstrate that architectures with 3 to 5 hidden layers, each with approximately 50 to 100 nodes, combined with one-hot encoding, achieve the best balance. In these configurations, neural networks accurately represent at least 97 % of the data, while compact lookup tables handle the remaining errors. The resulting Safety Nets reduce the system size by almost three orders of magnitude, fitting within the memory budget of current avionics hardware while guaranteeing 100 % correct outputs across the entire discretized input space, as required by EASA guidelines. This work provides the first-ever open-source implementation of Safety Nets for HCAS and VCAS with replicable results, demonstrating a practical pathway toward certifiable AI-based systems in aviation and establishing Safety Nets as a viable Safety-by-Design solution for safety-critical applications.

</details>


### [177] [What You Can't See Is What You Learn: Restricted Evidence Visibility Favors Compositional Generalization in Shared-Genome Language-Model Societies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20054)
*Narcis Marincat*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究测试了多模块系统中限制证据可见性是否会影响梯度训练发现的不同解决方案。发现限制可见性能够显著提高泛化继电器的概率，并促进可重用的值索引接口，但未完全通过预注册的标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模块系统通常将每个模块暴露于完整输入。研究者希望检验限制证据可见性是否会改变梯度训练所发现的不同解决方案，从而理解通信约束如何影响模块化系统的学习和泛化能力。

Method: 采用四单元社会网络架构，共享一个冻结的预训练语言模型和一个低秩适配器，仅通过固定中继中的两个模型宽度连续向量进行通信。在自然语言函数组合任务上训练十对匹配的受限/全局配对，共享初始化字节、训练顺序、标记布局、参数和计算，仅注意力掩码不同。进行了多种干预测试：切断通信、相同值数据包移植、破坏性干预和反事实数据包。

Result: 9/10对中受限社会在两个深度上均优于全局可见的对等体，优势至少20分，中位配对优势为0.7648（深度二）和0.6050（深度三）。切断通信使性能降至随机水平；深度三优势在对训练集中从未出现过的复合函数程序中保持0.558。相同值数据包移植在所有测试接口上保持行为（0.94-1.00），破坏性干预导致性能崩溃，反事实数据包将输出导向数学预测答案。唯一的高性能全局模型也需要通信，但其数据包在不同情境间不可互换。受限臂深度三中位准确率为0.6988，低于0.70门槛（未通过正式预注册）。早期合格队列0/10完全通过：一个模型满足所有任务性能门槛，但所有模型均未能保留普通语言。

Conclusion: 限制可见性对组合并非必要条件，但在此协议下，它显著增加了发现泛化继电器的概率，并倾向于形成可重用的值索引接口。然而，完整预注册测试电池形式上失败，系统被限制在显式任务门控使用范围内，因为所有模型在普通语言保留方面均未达标。

Abstract: Multi-module systems often expose every module to the full input. We test whether restricting evidence visibility changes which solutions gradient-based training discovers. Four-cell societies share one frozen pretrained language model and one low-rank adapter, communicating only through two model-width continuous vectors in a fixed relay. On a prospectively sealed natural-language function-composition task, we train ten matched restricted/global pairs sharing initialization bytes, training order, token layout, parameters, and computation; only the attention mask differs. Restricted societies outperform their globally visible twins by at least 20 points at both depths in 9 of 10 pairs, with median paired advantages of 0.7648 and 0.6050. Cutting communication reduces every restricted society to chance, and the depth-three advantage remains 0.558 on programs whose composite function never appeared in training. Across six audited restricted societies, same-value packet transplants preserve behavior at 0.94-1.00 across all tested interfaces; destructive interventions collapse performance; and counterfactual packets redirect outputs toward the mathematically predicted answer. The sole high-performing global model also requires communication, but its same-value packets are not interchangeable across episodes. Restricted visibility is thus not necessary for composition; under this protocol it substantially increases the probability of a generalizing relay and favors a reusable, value-indexed interface. The complete preregistered battery nevertheless formally fails because restricted-arm median depth-three accuracy is 0.6988, below the 0.70 floor. An earlier qualification cohort likewise yielded 0/10 complete passes: one model met every task-performance gate, but all ten failed ordinary-language preservation, confining the system to explicitly task-gated use.

</details>


### [178] [DARS: Dual-Level Credit Assignment RL with Structured Reasoning for Instruction-Based Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20161)
*Haoxiang Cao,Jiajiong Cao,Xuanpu Zhang,Changqian Yu,Chaoqun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DARS，一种针对指令式图像编辑中规划器-渲染器双阶段系统的强化学习框架，通过双层级信用分配机制，在保持相同计算预算下超越联合RL基线，尤其在推理密集型编辑任务上增益显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法仅依赖最终图像奖励进行训练，效率低下：劣质编辑结果无法区分应优化规划器还是渲染器，且规划器问题在自由形式推理链中难以定位。

Method: DARS采用多层级信用分配策略：模块层面，通过多计划多渲染滚动估计奖励变异性实现软模块路由，并利用滚动平均奖励构建自适应课程学习；规划器层面，使用四字段结构化推理输出，结合前缀门控奖励和词级优势重加权，将结果级反馈转化为局部监督信号。

Result: 在五个基准测试上，DARS在相同骨干网络、数据、奖励模型和滚动预算下均超越联合RL基线，推理密集型编辑任务提升最为显著。

Conclusion: DARS通过双层级信用分配机制有效解决了指令式图像编辑中的梯度归因问题，显著提升了规划器-渲染器管线的优化效率和最终性能。

Abstract: Instruction-based image editing uses a planner-renderer pipeline: a vision-language model (VLM) first converts the instruction into an edit plan, and a diffusion model then executes that plan. Training such systems with only final-image rewards is inefficient because a poor edit does not reveal whether additional optimization should place more emphasis on the planner or the renderer, and even planner-dominant cases remain difficult to localize within a free-form reasoning trace. We present DARS, a reinforcement learning framework for dual-level credit assignment in this two-stage setting. Across modules, multi-plan multi-render rollouts estimate between-plan and within-plan reward variability for soft module routing, while rollout mean rewards provide hardness estimates for an adaptive curriculum. Within the planner, a four-field structured reasoning output enables a prefix-gated reward and token-level advantage reweighting, turning outcome-level feedback into localized supervision. Experiments on five benchmarks show that DARS outperforms a Joint~RL baseline with the same backbone, data, reward model, and rollout budget, with the largest gains on reasoning-intensive edits.

</details>


### [179] [The Third Restructuring of Software Form: From the Three-Tier Architecture to Storage, Models, and Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20201)
*Wei Lin,Tao Zhou,Zhaofei Xie,Changgui Hong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出软件正在经历第三次范式转变（Software 3.0），其终态将收敛为三个核心要素：通用数据库（持久化状态与内存的统一抽象）、大模型（执行推理与生成的智能核心）和智能体（连接前两者的执行循环）。传统的三层架构中，用户界面层将被模型按需生成界面的能力所吸收，业务逻辑层将按"可表达性×关键性"重新划分为模型推理和存储约束，仅数据层被提升为唯一的持久化基础设施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着软件发展历经两次范式转变——Software 1.0（指令决定行为）和Software 2.0（数据决定行为/机器学习），本文旨在论证第三次范式转变正在发生，即Software 3.0（上下文和推理决定行为），并描述其终态形态及对软件产业的影响。

Method: 本文通过以下方式开展研究：1）形式化阐述收敛性论点；2）提出最小参考架构；3）基于真实原型和在线模型报告实证证据；4）系统分析该论点成立的条件及其失效边界。

Result: 该收敛性论点在可表达、可验证、外部有状态且工具完备的任务领域中成立。其失效边界包括确定性、成本、安全性和可验证性方面。在适用的领域内，传统三层架构将发生根本性重构：用户界面层被模型能力吸收，业务逻辑层被重新划分，数据层成为唯一持久化基础设施。

Conclusion: Software 3.0范式将重塑开发者角色、数据库行业和软件工程学科。传统的用户界面、业务逻辑和数据三层架构将向"通用数据库+大模型+智能体"三元架构收敛，其中界面由模型按需生成，业务逻辑按可表达性和关键性重新分配，数据层成为核心持久化基础设施。该转变在满足特定条件的任务领域内适用，并在确定性、成本、安全性和可验证性方面存在边界限制。

Abstract: Software form has undergone two paradigm shifts since its inception: Software 1.0, in which instructions determine behavior, and Software 2.0, in which data determines behavior (machine learning). This paper argues that a third shift - Software 3.0, in which context and reasoning determine behavior - is now underway, and contends that its terminal form converges to three elements: a generalized database (the unified abstraction of all persistent state and memory), a large model (the intelligence core that performs reasoning and generation), and an agent (the execution loop connecting the first two). The core argument is as follows: in the traditional three-tier architecture, the user-interface layer will be absorbed by the model's ability to generate interfaces on demand, the business-logic layer will be re-partitioned along "expressibility x criticality" into model reasoning and storage constraints (with residual deterministic logic retained as tools), and only the data layer will be elevated into the sole persistent infrastructure. We formalize this convergence thesis, present a minimal reference architecture, report evidence from real prototypes and a live model, and systematically analyze both the conditions under which it holds and the boundaries where it fails - determinism, cost, security, and verifiability delimit the thesis's domain of applicability. We argue that the thesis holds in task domains that are expressible, verifiable, externally stateful, and tool-complete, and that it will reshape the roles of developers, the database industry, and the software-engineering discipline.

</details>


### [180] [MemTrapBench: Benchmarking Cognitive Traps in LLM Memory Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20202)
*Mengru Wang,Haozhe Luo,Zhenqian Xu,Zhixiang Cui,Haoming Xu,Qu Yang,Jizhan Fang,Junfeng Fang,Ningyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了LLM中记忆引发的认知陷阱，并提出了MemTrapBench基准和AdaptiveMem方法以评估和缓解这些陷阱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆基准主要评估信息的提取、存储和检索，却忽视了检索到的记忆如何重塑模型推理并影响当前任务性能，导致记忆诱导的认知陷阱可能降低模型表现。因此，本文旨在系统评估这些失败模式并提出缓解方法。

Method: 我们提出了MemTrapBench基准，覆盖推理固着和信念扭曲两种认知陷阱形式；同时提出了AdaptiveMem，一种简单的推理时方法，通过指示LLM避免记忆陷阱来缓解认知陷阱。

Result: 在两个模型家族和五个代表性记忆框架上的实验表明，MemTrapBench具有挑战性：所有评估的记忆策略均低于无记忆设置，最强方法也下降超过10%。AdaptiveMem在MemTrapBench上缓解了认知陷阱，同时在标准记忆基准上保持或提升了性能。

Conclusion: 记忆诱导的认知陷阱会显著降低LLM性能，而AdaptiveMem提供了一种简单有效的缓解方案，在不损害记忆能力的前提下避免这些陷阱。

Abstract: Memory has become a key component of large language models, enabling them to retain information and learn from long-term interactions. However, existing memory benchmarks mainly evaluate whether information is correctly extracted, stored, and retrieved, while largely overlooking how retrieved memories reshape model reasoning and affect performance on the current task. We identify memory-induced cognitive traps: even faithfully recorded and semantically relevant memories can distort model reasoning or beliefs and degrade current task performance. To systematically evaluate these failure modes, we introduce MemTrapBench, which covers two forms of cognitive traps: Reasoning Fixation and Belief Distortion. Experiments across two model families and five representative memory frameworks show that MemTrapBench is challenging: all evaluated memory strategies underperform the no-memory setting, with even the strongest methods suffering drops of more than 10%. To mitigate these cognitive traps, we propose AdaptiveMem, a simple yet effective inference-time method that instructs LLMs to avoid memory traps. AdaptiveMem mitigates cognitive traps on MemTrapBench while preserving or improving performance on standard memory benchmarks across diverse memory frameworks.

</details>


### [181] [ContractScrub: A benchmark for final review of legal contracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20204)
*Yejin Bang,Kirsty Fielding,Brandan Oliver,Brian Birke,Nabeel Seedat,Andrew M. Bean*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了首个用于评估合同清理能力的基准测试ContractScrub，发现前沿模型在该任务上表现不佳，表明当前模型的实际局限性，并强调了针对特定领域的基准测试的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律工作大量依赖文本处理，合同清理作为常规且繁琐的工作，适合自动化，且与前沿大语言模型的长上下文推理、一致性检查和命名实体识别等能力自然契合。然而，此前缺乏对大语言模型进行合同清理能力的正式评估。

Method: 研究团队构建了ContractScrub基准测试，包含由经验丰富的律师手工制作的合同，涵盖误用定义术语、错误引用、语言不一致等多种错误类别，用于评估模型的合同清理能力。

Result: 前沿模型在ContractScrub基准上表现令人意外地差，仅有一个模型达到0.75的宏观平均召回率，尽管它们在看似相关的通用基准上表现强劲。

Conclusion: 当前模型在合同清理这一实际任务上存在局限性，强调需要针对特定领域设计窄向基准测试，以准确衡量大语言模型的实际影响。

Abstract: Legal work, with its heavy reliance on processing large amounts of text, is often considered one of the domains most exposed to the use of LLMs. Contract ``scrubbing,'' the final review of transactional agreements for errors and inconsistencies, is a particularly suitable task for automation, because it is routine, painstaking work requiring detailed attention to long documents. Scrubbing also seems to align naturally with the general capabilities expected of frontier LLMs around long-context reasoning, consistency checking, and named entity recognition (NER). Despite the economic value and potential for automation, no formal evaluations of LLMs performing contract scrubbing have been conducted. We introduce ContractScrub, the first benchmark designed to evaluate contract scrubbing capabilities, comprising contracts hand-crafted by experienced lawyers over diverse error categories such as misuse of defined terms, incorrect references, and inconsistent language. Frontier models perform surprisingly poorly with only one model reaching 0.75 macro average recall despite strong performance on seemingly related general benchmarks, demonstrating the practical limits of current models and the importance of narrowly targeted, domain-specific benchmarks for measuring real-world impact.

</details>


### [182] [Electronic Navigational Chart Change Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20218)
*Jacob Arndt,Abhishek Potnis,Alexandre Sorokine*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自动化分类电子海图(ENC)变更的方法，通过构建编码方案将复杂的矢量数据变更转化为结构化表格格式，并引入空间上下文编码器和ENC属性编码器，在两个真实数据集上实现了90%和94%的准确率，优于基线模型5-7%，证明了机器学习在海图维护中的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有海图变更风险评估依赖人工审核，存在劳动密集、扩展性差、分析师间不一致等问题，亟需自动化解决方案以提高海事安全。

Method: 设计编码方案将矢量数据变更转化为结构化表格，包含空间上下文编码器（富化变更表示的周围地理特征）和ENC属性编码器（表示修改对象的属性值描述），并使用梯度提升树模型进行分类。

Result: 在两个操作数据集（1308对海图，超100,000次变更）上，调优的梯度提升树模型准确率达90%和94%，比无空间上下文和属性嵌入的默认模型提高5-7%。

Conclusion: 机器学习可有效整合到运营地理空间管道中，改进海图维护并增强海事安全；简单位置和空间聚合方法的有效性为更复杂的空间表示学习技术奠定了基础。

Abstract: Electronic Navigational Charts (ENCs) are geospatial vector datasets used in maritime navigation systems that represent hydrographic and navigational information such as depths, navigational aids, traffic schemes, and hazards. A major challenge for hydrographic offices is determining whether a given chart change poses a critical or non-critical risk to maritime safety. Existing workflows rely heavily on manual review and verification, which is labor-intensive, scales poorly with the volume of incoming chart updates, and introduces inter-analyst inconsistencies. To address this challenge, we propose a method for automated classification of ENC changes. We establish a baseline encoding scheme to translate complex vector data changes into a structured tabular format for classification models. The two crucial components of the encoding scheme include a spatial context encoder to enrich the change representations with surrounding geographic features, and an ENC attribute encoder to represent nuanced attribute-value descriptions of the modified objects. We evaluate the proposed approach across two distinct operational datasets, comprising 1,308 chart pairs containing over 100,000 individual chart modifications. Tuned gradient-boosted trees leveraging the proposed encoding schemes achieve accuracies of 90% and 94% on the two datasets, yielding a 5-7% improvement over default hyperparameterized models trained on encodings without spatial context and attribute embeddings. These results demonstrate the viability of integrating machine learning into operational geospatial pipelines to improve ENC maintenance and enhance maritime safety. Finally, our experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of simple location and spatial aggregation methods, providing a foundation for evaluating more sophisticated spatial representation learning techniques for this application.

</details>


### [183] [InsufficiencyBench: Evaluating LLM legal advice on underspecified user queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20220)
*Samuel J. Vincent,Daniel Calloway,Fangyi Yu,Andrew M. Bean,Nabeel Seedat*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了InsufficiencyBench，这是第一个专门评估法律AI系统处理信息不完整查询能力的基准测试。研究发现当前前沿模型在识别缺失法律要素方面表现不佳，F2分数最高仅为0.46，且存在无差别谨慎或基于虚构假设作答的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律AI系统在实际应用中面临用户查询信息不完整的问题，现有基准测试未考虑这种"查询侧不足"情况。当用户省略对法律结果有实质影响的关键事实时，AI系统需要能够识别信息缺失、指出遗漏内容并避免过早下结论。

Method: 研究者构建了InsufficiencyBench基准测试，定义了八类典型缺失要素体系结构和三种结构性失败模式（切换、门控、致命前提）。创建了202个基准项目（58个基础查询，144个缺陷变体），覆盖6个法律领域和24个美国司法管辖区，并由执业律师进行标注。对十个前沿法律AI模型进行了系统评估。

Result: 评估结果显示所有模型在缺失要素识别任务上表现欠佳：F2分数均低于0.46，中位召回率仅0.44。模型主要呈现两种失败模式：一是无差别地采取谨慎态度而失去有效性；二是在缺乏依据的情况下做出确定性回答。没有任何模型能够在正确识别不完整查询并限定回答的同时，又能直接有效处理完整查询。

Conclusion: 当前法律AI系统在处理信息不完整查询方面存在显著局限，特别是在识别缺失法律要素和适当限定回答方面。这表明实际应用场景中的法律AI应用仍面临重大挑战，需要进一步改进模型对查询完整性的判断能力和相应响应策略。

Abstract: Legal AI systems are increasingly used to answer legal questions, yet existing benchmarks assume queries arrive fully specified. In practice, users omit facts that materially determine the legal outcome. We introduce InsufficiencyBench, the first legal benchmark targeting query-side insufficiency: whether a model recognizes when a query lacks legally material information, identifies what is missing, and refrains from premature conclusions. We formalize a taxonomy of eight canonical missing-element categories across three structural failure modes---switch, gating, and fatal prerequisite--- and construct 202 benchmark items (58 base queries, 144 deficient variants) spanning six legal domains and 24 US jurisdictions and annotated by practising attorneys. Evaluating ten frontier models, we find that no model exceeds F2 = 0.46 on missing-element identification and that the median recall is 0.44. Models either hedge indiscriminately or answer silently under fabricated presumptions. No model both identifies and qualifies responses to deficient queries while directly addressing complete ones.

</details>


### [184] [Rule-Compliant Visual Spatial Planning for Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20237)
*Yu Chen,Ting Lei,Yaoyi Li,Jia Cai,Zhecen Wu,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RuleMaze基准和DMP方法，用于评估多模态大语言模型在自然语言规则约束下的视觉空间规划能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型虽结合了语言推理和视觉感知，但在显式或未见规则约束下进行视觉空间规划的能力尚未充分探索，需要模型联合理解空间布局、解释自然语言规则并规划有效动作。

Method: 提出RuleMaze可控基准，要求模型在遵守不同复杂度自然语言规则的情况下导航迷宫；提出语言-逻辑-函数混合方法自动生成规则并转换为逻辑表示和可执行验证器；提出解耦多模态规划(DMP)方法，通过可解释的推理原语分离感知、执行和规则验证。

Result: 实验表明，与端到端文本规划基线相比，DMP显著提高了规则遵守和规划成功率。

Conclusion: RuleMaze为研究多模态大语言模型中接地且可解释的规则基础空间规划建立了基准，代码已开源。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) combine linguistic reasoning with visual perception, yet their ability to perform visual spatial planning under explicit or previously unseen rule constraints remains underexplored. This setting requires models to jointly understand spatial layouts, interpret natural-language rules, and plan valid actions accordingly. To address this gap, we introduce RuleMaze, a controllable benchmark in which MLLMs must navigate mazes while obeying natural-language rules of varying complexity. RuleMaze isolates rule-compliant spatial planning by requiring accurate perception, rule interpretation, and constrained action planning. To enable scalable and systematic rule construction, we propose Language-Logic-Function Hybridization, which automatically generates natural-language rules and translates them into logical representations and executable validators, eliminating manual rule engineering. To improve rule following and generalization, we introduce Disentangled Multimodal Planning (DMP), which separates perception, execution, and rule verification through interpretable reasoning primitives. By disentangling these components, DMP facilitates systematic generalization to more complex and previously unseen rules, while providing transparent intermediate planning traces. Experiments demonstrate that DMP substantially improves rule compliance and planning success compared to end-to-end textual planning baselines. Overall, RuleMaze establishes a principled benchmark for studying grounded and interpretable rule-based spatial planning in MLLMs. Code is available at https://github.com/oceanflowlab/RuleMaze.

</details>


### [185] [QUASAR: A Quantum-Classical Neural Network for SAR Satellite Physical-Layer Authentication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20240)
*Vincenzo Sammartino,Nathanael Denis,Roberto Di Pietro*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: X-band SAR satellites (8-12 GHz) play a critical role in disaster response, environmental monitoring, and military intelligence. Yet, they lack robust physical-layer authentication (PLA), a security layer orthogonal to cryptographic solutions. Existing PLA systems, typically based on radio-frequency fingerprinting, are often limited to sub-6 GHz frequencies and rely on classical deep learning. However, this approach underfits the IQ phase nonlinearities that distinguish satellite hardware. In this paper, we present QUASAR, to the best of our knowledge the first quantum-classical hybrid architecture that fuses a CNN spectrogram encoder with a variational quantum circuit (VQC) to provide PLA to X-band SAR signals. Our solution enjoys two distinctive features: (i) it is markedly more data-efficient than classical machine learning, requiring only 10% of the training data to match the accuracy of classical baselines -- data collection being notoriously the most time-consuming phase of PLA; and, (ii) at an equal data budget, it improves classification accuracy over those baselines. In detail, we test our solution under three adversarial scenarios: replay, crafted-IQ injection, and space-borne spoofing. QUASAR rejects spoofed transmissions in 89.7%, 94.1%, and 81.3% of attempts, respectively, establishing the first quantum-enhanced physical-layer classifier for satellite constellations. The fully detailed framework and the supporting results, other than being interesting on their own, show a novel research avenue for physical-layer authentication.

</details>


### [186] [Learning When to Think: Adaptive Reasoning for Test-Time Compute Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20256)
*Gijs Kassenaar,Zhao Yang,Vincent François-Lavet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种自适应推理模式选择机制，让语言模型根据问题难度动态选择NoThink/Short/Long三种推理模式，在保证准确率的同时将平均响应长度减少41%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前推理语言模型采用固定token预算策略，导致简单问题过度计算资源浪费，而复杂问题又因计算不足影响解答质量，缺乏基于问题难度的动态资源分配能力。

Method: 在Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)框架内集成模式选择机制，通过设计分层奖励函数使不同模式在相应响应长度下具有最优性，并设置硬性token上限确保各模式特征区分，无需额外路由模块即可实现端到端训练。

Result: 在1.5B蒸馏模型MATH数据集上成功形成三种差异化推理模式，简短模式准确率优于长推理模式；在MATH500测试集上准确率保持0.782（基准0.796），平均响应长度从4,796降至2,811 tokens（减少41%）；无微调条件下可迁移至其他基准，GSM8K实现76% token缩减且准确率优于同等长度基线。

Conclusion: 验证了语言模型能够有效学习基于问题难度的自适应推理策略，该框架通过动态调整计算投入实现了计算效率与推理质量的平衡，为资源受限场景下的智能体部署提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Reasoning language models trained with reinforcement learning typically operate under a fixed token budget rather than an explicitly adaptive one, which can lead to over-computation on easy problems and insufficient computation on difficult ones. We study whether a model can learn to allocate its own reasoning effort by choosing, as the first token of its response, one of three modes: \textsc{NoThink} (answer as quickly as possible), \textsc{Short} (brief reasoning), or \textsc{Long} (extended reasoning). The choice is learned inside Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with no separate router, through a shaped reward that makes each mode worthwhile at a different response length, together with hard per-mode token caps that keep the modes distinct. On a 1.5B distilled model trained on MATH, the three modes emerge without collapsing to a single choice, and the brief modes end up more accurate than \textsc{Long}, which shows that the router sorts problems by difficulty rather than at random. Averaged over three seeds, the resulting policy stays close to the base model's accuracy on the held-out MATH500 ($0.782$ vs.\ $0.796$) while cutting the mean response length from $4{,}796$ to $2{,}811$ tokens (a $41\%$ reduction). Interestingly, it also transfers to other benchmarks without retraining, with the largest savings where problems are easier, with for instance 76\% token reduction on GSM8K and at higher accuracy than the baselines at similar response length. In short, we build a reasoning model that adaptively chooses how much to reason for each problem.

</details>


### [187] [Catching the Rug: Early Prediction of Fraudulent Memecoins on Solana via Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20271)
*Jianghai Li,Pavel Kuznetsov,Yury Yanovich,Konstantin Nott-Whaley,Igor Vodolazov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了Solana生态系统中memecoin的rug pull早期检测问题。研究者构建了包含640万个代币、历时7个月的数据集，发现大多数rug pull在上线一小时内即显现特征。利用梯度提升算法（XGBoost），仅使用最初5分钟的交易数据即可实现稳健的rug pull检测。此外，通过多源数据融合有效缓解了跨平台域偏移问题，提升了检测可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区块链平台上memecoin的迅速泛滥增加了欺诈活动风险，特别是rug pull事件。以往研究主要关注以太坊代币，而Solana已成为按交易量和代币数量计算的主要memecoin区块链。与以太坊中利用智能合约后门实施rug pull不同，Solana memecoin rug pull主要由流动性操纵和社会动态驱动。亟需针对Solana生态系统的rug pull早期检测方法。

Method: 研究者构建了包含640万个代币、历时7个月的大规模数据集。采用经典机器学习模型，特别是梯度提升算法（XGBoost），仅使用最初5分钟的交易数据进行rug pull检测。评估了PumpFun和Raydium之间的跨平台泛化能力，并通过多源数据融合缓解域偏移问题。

Result: 绝大多数memecoin在上线一小时内即表现出rug pull特征，凸显了短期预测的紧迫性。梯度提升算法（XGBoost）使用仅5分钟交易数据即可实现稳健的rug pull检测性能。多源数据融合显著缓解了域偏移问题并提高了检测可靠性。

Conclusion: 本研究推进了对高吞吐量链上DeFi欺诈的理解，为保护投资者提供了实用框架。率先实现了Solana生态系统中的大规模rug pull早期检测。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of memecoins on blockchain platforms has increased the risk of fraudulent activities, particularly rug pulls. While previous studies have focused on Ethereum-based tokens, this paper shifts the spotlight to Solana, the leading blockchain for memecoins by trading volume and token count. Unlike Ethereum, where rug pulls often exploit smart contract backdoors, Solana memecoin rug pulls are predominantly driven by liquidity manipulation and social dynamics. This research pioneers large-scale rug pull early detection in the Solana ecosystem by assembling a dataset of 6.4 million tokens over 7 months. Market analysis reveals that a vast majority of these memecoins exhibit rug pull characteristics within one hour of launch, highlighting the urgency of short-horizon prediction. Despite the absence of code-level features, we demonstrate that classic machine learning models, particularly Gradient Boosting (XGBoost), achieve robust performance in detecting potential rug pulls using only the first 5 minutes of trading data. Furthermore, we evaluate cross-platform generalization between PumpFun and Raydium, revealing that multi-source data fusion significantly mitigates domain shift and improves detection reliability. This study advances the understanding of DeFi fraud on high-throughput chains and provides a practical framework for protecting investors.

</details>


### [188] [Break It Down, Pass It On: Cross-Task Skill Transfer in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20274)
*Yiyang Feng,Biddut Sarker Bijoy,Niranjan Balasubramanian,Jiawei Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型（LLM）代理诱导技能在不同任务间的迁移效果，对比了任务级与子任务级诱导方式及文本与代码格式，并提出结合特异性和抽象性的“技能效用评分”以预测任务成功概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理虽能通过经验诱导并重用技能以提升能力，但诱导出的技能迁移可能不可靠甚至损害性能。技能在何种条件下能可靠迁移仍是未解之谜，需探究技能诱导方式如何影响跨任务迁移效果。

Method: 进行受控研究，对比任务级与子任务级诱导以及文本与代码格式；考察诱导技能的“特异性”（与真实任务匹配度）和“抽象性”（相关性分布均匀度），并据此构建“技能效用评分”模型。

Result: 任务级技能通常导致性能低于无记忆基线，子任务级技能平均性能高于基线；文本技能迁移优于代码技能；技能效用评分与迁移时的任务成功率高度相关，子任务级和文本技能在该评分上更高。

Conclusion: 子任务级和文本格式的技能更有效。提出的技能效用评分无需任务执行，仅需技能和任务描述即可计算，可作为执行新任务前诊断技能记忆质量的实用工具。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents can induce skills from completed tasks and reuse them later to grow more capable with experience. In practice, induced skills may transfer unreliably and can even harm the agent that retrieves them. When agent-induced skills transfer reliably across tasks remains an open question. We conduct a comprehensive and controlled study of how the way skills are induced shapes their transfer across tasks. Specifically, we compare task-level with subtask-level skill induction and text with code skill formats, the two axes along which existing methods differ. Task-level skills mostly reduce the agent's performance below its no-memory baseline while subtask-level skills raise it above on average, and text skills transfer better than code skills. To further understand our findings, we examine two complementary properties of the induced skills: specificity, which measures how closely a skill matches real tasks, and abstractness, which measures how evenly its relevance spreads across tasks. Neither property alone predicts task success, but their combined effect does, which we propose as a skill utility score. The score correlates consistently with task success when skills are transferred, and subtask-level and text skills score higher. Computing skill utility only needs the skills and task descriptions but not any task execution, so our score serves as a practical diagnostic of a skill memory before any new task runs.

</details>


### [189] [Phantom Gains: Auditing Self-Improvement Against a Measured Null](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20290)
*Cheng Xu,Nan Yan,Liming Chen,M-Tahar Kechadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文审计了语言模型改进追踪中的测量伪影，发现七种标准实践失败会在无控制时反转研究结论，并提出了一种稳健的逐问题精确检验方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前追踪语言模型在单个问题上改进的方法（通过差分两个噪声估计值）易受测量伪影影响，导致关于模型是否自我改进的结论不可靠。

Method: 作者对Qwen3-8B上的三轮秩-32 LoRA自训练与冻结控制组进行了审计，识别出七种测量失败。他们提出用基于汇总基线的逐问题精确检验替代自然阈值修复，并在错误发现率控制下应用该方法。

Result: 审计发现七种测量失败均会在无控制时反转研究结论。新检验方法在留出版本上未检测到任何改进。外部蒸馏能改进基线模型几乎无法解决的问题，而自训练不能；回归分析表明该不对称性是蒸馏总体增益的副产品。自训练以远高于测量底限的速率破坏基线已解决的问题。

Conclusion: 过渡级审计需要对报告的每个统计量分别测量零假设，这些零假设可从多臂研究已有的基线复制中构建，尽管不能像大多数研究那样仅用少量数据。

Abstract: Whether a language model has improved itself is increasingly judged not by mean accuracy but by which individual problems it gains and loses. Tracking these transitions means differencing two noisy estimates, leaving them vulnerable to measurement artifacts. Auditing three rounds of rank-$32$ LoRA self-training on Qwen3-8B against a frozen control pushed through the identical pipeline, we identify seven measurement failures, each of which inverts a reported finding when its control is absent. Several are standard practice. A ledger built on a single greedy decode manufactures capability changes on an untrained model, largely an artifact of inference batching; the expansion statistic separating acquisition from sharpening assigns that same model a rate of $0.280$. The natural threshold repair does not survive replication: estimated across the frozen comparisons such a design already contains, its null stays non-zero. We replace it with a per-problem exact test against a pooled baseline under false-discovery-rate control, which detects nothing on any held-out replicate and is unchanged under the multiple-testing rule, error rate and pool size. Applied to a ladder of arms matched in stream, volume and evaluation, the audit finds that external distillation improves problems the base model rarely reaches while three forms of self-training do not; a regression rejects this asymmetry as a by-product of distillation's larger overall gain ($p < 10^{-8}$). On the far smaller set of problems the base model never reaches, the evidence is inconclusive, while self-training corrupts problems solved at baseline at rates well above the measured floor. Transition-level auditing therefore requires a separately measured null for every statistic it reports: nulls that cost no new experiments, built from baseline replicates a multi-arm study already owns, though not from as few as most possess.

</details>


### [190] [MidTool: Mid-training Data Synthesis for Agentic Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20314)
*Fengqing Jiang,Yite Wang,Boyi Liu,Zhaoyang Wang,Canwen Xu,Zhewei Yao,Radha Poovendran,Yuxiong He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MidTool——一个用于智能体工具使用中期训练的开源语料构建管线，结合大规模网络/PDF/代码数据与真实工具API合成监督。在Qwen3-4B/8B-Base上进行中期训练后，配合监督微调和强化学习，在BFCL、tau2-Bench和MCP Universe基准上均取得一致性能提升，表明通用工具使用能力需要专门的中期训练支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中期训练已被认为是塑造大语言模型能力的关键阶段，已有工作证明定向中期训练可增强数学、科学等推理密集能力以及软件工程场景下的智能体能力。然而，通用工具使用这一平行的智能体能力尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 构建MidTool语料管线，整合大规模网络、PDF、代码数据，并从真实工具API、MCP技能和文档驱动工作流中生成合成监督信号。该管线旨在教会模型识别工具可用性、从上下文中提取参数、组合工具调用工作流以及从不完整信息中恢复。随后在Qwen3-4B-Base和Qwen3-8B-Base上进行中期训练，并应用包含监督微调和强化学习的后续训练。

Result: MidTool-Mix在BFCL、tau2-Bench和MCP Universe基准上，无论是在监督微调还是强化学习设置下，均相比基线方法实现了持续的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通用工具使用与其他重要的LLM能力类似，需要专门的中期训练支持，而非完全依赖后续训练阶段来解决。

Abstract: Mid-training is increasingly recognized as a critical stage for shaping the capabilities of large language models. Recent work has shown that targeted mid-training can strengthen reasoning-intensive abilities such as math and science, and can also improve agentic capabilities in software-engineering settings. In this work, we study the parallel but less explored agentic capability: general tool use. We present MidTool, an open corpus construction pipeline for agentic tool-use mid-training that combines large-scale web, PDF, and code data with synthesized supervision from real-world tool APIs, MCP skills, and document-grounded workflows. MidTool is designed to teach models how to recognize tool affordances, ground arguments from context, compose tool call workflow, and recover from incomplete information. We mid-train Qwen3-4B-Base and Qwen3-8B-Base on MidTool-Mix, and then apply follow-up post-training with both supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning. Compared with baselines, MidTool-Mix consistently improves downstream performance under both SFT and RL on BFCL, tau2-Bench, and MCP Universe. These results suggest that general tool use, like other important LLM capabilities, benefits from dedicated mid-training rather than being left entirely to post-training.

</details>


### [191] [Pandora's AI Model Routing Box: Efficient Allocation with Costly Value Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20316)
*Adam Fisch,Shubhendu Trivedi,Fantine Huot,William W. Cohen,Michael Kaisers,Mirella Lapata,Kate Larson,Jacob Eisenstein*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过引入潘多拉魔盒问题框架，解决了异构AI系统中查询路由的价值估计成本与准确性之间的权衡问题，提出了集中式与分布式两种路由策略，在保证路由质量的同时显著降低昂贵估计器的调用频率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构AI系统由多个模型、架构或推理设置组成，需要通过路由将查询分配给最具效性的"专家"以兼顾质量与成本。然而，评估每个专家预期回报的价值估计本身存在成本：廉价估计器（如基于嵌入的预测器）快速但噪声大，准确估计器（如带有检索结果或部分推理轨迹的微调模型）准确但昂贵。这种成本-准确性权衡需要系统性的处理方案。

Method: 将价值估计成本与准确性的权衡形式化为潘多拉魔盒问题（经典的最优搜索与成本检验问题）。在高斯信号模型下推导闭式价值信息表达式，为每个专家和输入确定是否值得付出成本细化价值估计。提出集中式策略"潘多拉路由器"和分布式策略"潘多拉竞标者"（专家独立决定是否投入自我评估成本以接受报价）。在三个领域进行实验验证：标准多LLM基准、检索增强专家、具有可变推理时间的LLM。

Result: 实验显示，潘多拉路由器达到了与穷举估计相同的 路由质量，而调用昂贵估计器的频率大幅降低。在分布式设置中，当竞争估计准确时，价值信息推理提升了配置效率；但当竞争估计噪声大时，反而可能以增加战略专家效用为代价损害其他专家利益。

Conclusion: 潘多拉魔盒框架为异构AI系统中的查询路由提供了 principled 的理论基础，实现价值估计成本与路由质量的最优平衡。集中式策略在减少昂贵估计调用方面表现卓越，分布式策略在估计准确条件下提升效率，但在噪声环境下存在博弈论层面的挑战。该研究为异构AI系统的资源分配提供了可操作的解决方案。

Abstract: Heterogeneous AI systems composed of multiple models, architectures, harnesses, or inference-time settings can improve quality and efficiency by routing queries to the specialist who can answer most effectively at the lowest cost. Routing requires estimating each specialist's expected return, but this value estimation has a cost. Cheap estimators (e.g., embedding-based predictors) are fast but noisy, while accurate estimators (e.g., fine-tuned models with access to retrieval results or partial reasoning traces) are expensive. We formalize this tradeoff as an instance of Pandora's Box, the classical problem of optimal search with costly inspection. Under a Gaussian signal model, the resulting policies have closed-form value-of-information expressions that determine, for each specialist and input, whether refining the value estimate is worth its cost. We call the centralized policy Pandora's Router. We extend this to a decentralized setting, Pandora's Bidder, where specialists independently decide whether to invest in self-assessment before accepting an offered price to claim a query. Experiments across three domains---a standard multi-LLM benchmark, retrieval-augmented specialists, and LLMs with variable inference-time reasoning---show that Pandora's Router matches the routing quality of exhaustive estimation, while querying the expensive estimator far less often. In the decentralized setting, value-of-information reasoning improves allocative efficiency when competing estimates are accurate; when competing estimates are noisy, however, it can increase the strategic specialist's utility at the expense of others.

</details>


### [192] [AI4AI-Bench: Benchmarking LLM Agents in Algorithmic Design for Recursive Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20318)
*Yizhe Chi,Wenyi Li,Deyao Hong,Xiaoqiu Wang,Mingju Gao,Kaisen Yang,Bingxiang He,Youjie Zheng,Calvin Xiao,Qinhuai Na*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本论文创建了一个评估AI系统设计训练算法能力的基准测试AI4AI-Bench，发现即使是最强的系统也只能实现有限改进，主要障碍在于系统是否愿意改变模型学习方式而非技术能力本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归自我改进(RSI)的核心问题是AI系统能否改进产生AI系统的过程，特别是训练算法。现有基准测试无法分离评估这种能力，因为它们主要奖励数据收集或超参数调整，而非改变模型学习方式。

Method: 提出AI4AI-Bench，包含10个冻结研究仓库覆盖10种训练算法家族。每个任务中，agent有4小时在B300上重写训练算法，代码然后从头运行最多12小时，由对agent隐藏的固定评估器评分。所有任务映射到统一尺度：0为无信息模型，0.1为仓库原有算法，1.0为任务最优。

Result: 在29种配置、6个系统、10个任务中，平均得分0.166，最佳系统达0.250。大多数提交从未改变模型学习方式；改变的少数平均0.226，未改变的平均0.126。更多推理努力主要提升了改变意愿(从8%到64%)和平均得分(从0.094到0.196)。

Conclusion: 发布任务套件、评估器和所有评分提交以支持可重复测量。结果显示即使最强系统也仅覆盖了现有算法与最优之间不到五分之一的距离，表明在AI系统设计训练算法能力方面存在显著改进空间，主要瓶颈在于改变学习方式的意愿而非技术能力。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement (RSI) asks whether an AI system can improve the process that produces AI systems, so that the next system inherits the improvement. That process is the training algorithm: a better objective or update rule improves the compute\mbox{-}capability exchange rate for every subsequent run, including the one that produces the next agent. Whether RSI is feasible therefore turns on whether an agent can design training algorithms. No benchmark isolates that ability: existing suites are won by collecting data or by tuning hyperparameters, and none tells a change to how a run is executed apart from a change to how the model learns. We present AI4AI\mbox{-}Bench, 10 frozen research repositories spanning 10 training algorithm families. In each task, an agent has 4 hours on one B300 to rewrite the training algorithm; its code is then rerun from scratch for up to 12 hours and scored by a fixed evaluator hidden from the agent, against the repository's original algorithm under the same procedure. Because the 10 metrics are incommensurable, every task is mapped onto one scale on which $0$ is an uninformative model, $0.1$ is the algorithm the repository ships, and $1.0$ is the task optimum. Across 29 configurations of 6 systems on all 10 tasks the mean score is $0.166$, and the best system reaches $0.250$: even the strongest closes under a fifth of the distance between the algorithm that was already there and the optimum. The submissions show where that distance went: most never change how the model learns at all, and the minority that do average $0.226$ against $0.126$ for the rest. More reasoning effort mostly buys the willingness to go there, taking that minority from $8\%$ of submissions to $64\%$ and the mean score from $0.094$ to $0.196$. We release the task suite, the evaluators and every scored submission, so that the measurement can be repeated as these systems change.

</details>


### [193] [An Agentic Approach for Active Data Collection, Travel Behavior Modeling, and Weather-Sensitive Demand Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20320)
*Narges Ahmadi,Yubo Jiao,Jônatas Augusto Manzolli,Jiangbo Yu,Luis Miranda-Moreno*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出并验证了一种三智能体工作流，将对话式数据采集（聊天机器人+图像增强）、结构化数据建模（多项Logit/机器学习）与多模态大语言模型（LLM）行为预测整合，证明视觉上下文可提升交通方式选择预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常将数字数据采集与预测建模作为独立阶段开发和评估，缺乏端到端协同框架。本研究旨在解决这一割裂问题，通过构建可审计的多智能体工作流实现从数据采集到预测的无缝衔接。

Method: 1. **数据采集**：部署聊天机器人执行图像增强陈述性偏好调查，收集5种天气场景下学生通勤者的出行选择（454条观测）
2. **数据处理**：使用多项Logit模型分析天气关联，随机森林与逻辑回归作为机器学习基准
3. **LLM评估**：在零样本提示条件下测试9个本地部署LLM（2B-35B参数），扩展至人格化、少样本及视觉配置
4. **对比分析**：比较传统模型、文本LLM及视觉增强LLM在五分类任务中的表现

Result: 1. **精度排名**：视觉增强LLM(71.5%) > 零样本文本LLM(69.9%) ≈ 随机森林(69.6%)
2. **关键发现**：
   - 习惯性出行信息提供最大增益
   - "专家框架"优于"角色扮演"
   - 少样本提示在少量示例后趋于稳定
   - 视觉上下文对部分模型提供增量预测价值

Conclusion: 本研究首次系统验证了对话式调查、结构化建模、传统机器学习和多模态LLM预测的可协同工作流。结果表明：
1. 多模态LLM在交通行为预测中具有竞争力
2. 视觉特征可补充文本信息的局限性
3. 智能体间协作可提升模型鲁棒性
4. 该框架为可审计的交通决策支持系统提供了新范式

Abstract: Travel behavior research increasingly combines digital data collection with predictive modeling, yet these stages are often developed and evaluated separately. This study proposes a three-agent workflow integrating conversational data collection, structured data processing, and behavioral prediction. A chatbot-administered, image-augmented stated-preference survey collected mode choices from student commuters across five predefined weather scenarios, yielding 454 respondent-scenario observations. Weather-related associations were analyzed using a multinomial logit model, while logistic regression and random forest provided machine-learning benchmarks. Nine locally deployed large language models (LLMs), ranging from 2 to 35 billion parameters, were evaluated across four zero-shot prompt-and-context conditions and extended through persona, few-shot, and vision-based configurations. Random forest achieved 69.6% five-class accuracy, while the best text-only zero-shot LLM reached 69.9% without task-specific fitting. Habitual travel information produced the most consistent gains, Expert framing generally outperformed Role-Play, and persona information was most useful when habitual travel information was unavailable. Few-shot prompting improved prediction for several models, with gains stabilizing after a small number of examples. Using the same weather images shown to respondents, the best vision-based configuration reached 71.5% five-class accuracy, indicating that visual context may provide additional predictive information for selected models. Overall, the study shows how conversational surveys, structured data processing, conventional behavioral modeling, machine learning, and multimodal LLM prediction can be coordinated within an auditable multi-agent workflow.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [194] [Online Scheduling for Throughput Maximization of Time-varying Markovian Channels with Unknown Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19398)
*Tasmeen Zaman Ornee,Clement Kam,Ness B. Shroff*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出基于AoCSI的在线无线调度算法Online-MGF，在未知信道统计下实现sub-linear regret，并证明ON/OFF信道的Whittle index可解性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统belief state方法依赖信道时间相关性统计，但实际场景中信道统计常未知导致状态空间爆炸；同时CSI获取成本高且易过时

Method: 创新性引入AoCSI作为CSI新鲜度度量，证明最新CSI与AoCSI构成充分统计量；将问题建模为RMAB并设计Online-MGF策略，通过在线学习避免显式状态估计

Result: 1) 在线算法实现sub-linear regret；2) 对ON/OFF信道证明indexability并推导Whittle index闭式解；3) 数值验证显示Online-MGF仅需少量episode即可收敛至已知统计下的最优策略

Conclusion: AoCSI框架有效压缩状态空间，所提算法在资源受限场景下兼具理论保证与实际可行性，为未知环境下的动态资源分配提供新范式

Abstract: We consider a wireless scheduling problem in downlink wireless networks with unknown channel statistics, where a Base Station (BS) sends data to multiple users. The scheduling performance relies heavily on accurate Channel State Information (CSI), which is often costly to acquire. In this paper, CSI is obtained from ACK/NACK feedback, only after each scheduled transmission. Due to limited wireless channel resources, all users cannot be scheduled for transmission simultaneously. Hence, the most recently observed CSI can be outdated. The traditional approach to solve scheduling problems using outdated CSI is to utilize belief states, which are calculated using the time correlation statistics of channels. However, channel statistics are often unknown; consequently, belief states can be uncountable and this approach becomes infeasible. In this paper, we introduce a new sufficient statistics for the wireless scheduling problem. Towards this effort, we characterize the CSI staleness by the Age of Channel State Information (AoCSI) and show that the latest observed CSI and its AoCSI is a sufficient statistic of the history to make the scheduling decisions. Accordingly, we are able to reduce the state space for online learning. Our goal is to develop an online scheduling algorithm that maximizes the expected sum throughput of all users over a finite time-horizon while satisfying a channel resource constraint. The formulated problem is a Restless Multi-armed Bandit (RMAB). We develop an online Maximum Gain First (Online-MGF) policy, which achieves sub-linear regret on the number of episodes. For a special case of ON/OFF channels, we are able to prove indexability and derive a closed-form expression of the Whittle index. Numerical results demonstrate that the Online-MGF policy converges to MGF and Whittle index policies with known statistics within a very few episodes.

</details>


### [195] [Empirical Evaluation of Cross-Carrier MCPTT & OTT MCX Interoperability in High-Density Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19554)
*Eman Hammad,Derek Ladd,Sridhar Kowdley,Walt Magnussen,Michael Fox*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 在大型人群事件期间，基于共享商业网络部署关键任务一键通服务会引发资源竞争。本研究发现语音服务故障源于网络基础设施瓶颈而非设备硬件，存在一个非线性的网络故障模型，即传输层抖动超过临界阈值会导致缓冲器欠载，造成音频结构性退化；而优先级管理的信道能有效绕过拥塞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模人群事件的多机构应急响应中，宽带关键任务一键通服务部署于共享商业基础设施上会引发资源竞争，亟需评估跨运营商互操作性以及标准与优先级QoS框架在真实饱和约束下的表现。

Method: 设计了一项经验性多运营商现场实验，在德克萨斯农工大学凯尔球场一场有10.5万+观众的美式足球比赛期间，将12部相同的智能手机部署在多个物理区域内，使用感知客观聆听质量分析（POLQA）、数据包传输指标和连接率对自动语音通话进行监控。

Result: 语音路径故障被隔离在网络基础设施瓶颈而非设备硬件限制上。识别出一个陡峭的非线性网络故障模型：当传输层抖动超过临界阈值时，去抖动缓冲器欠载，导致音频结构性退化。优先级管理信道能有效绕过此拥塞。

Conclusion: 本研究为紧急规划者建立了操作性见解：应强制要求网络基础设施、端到端网络切片和专用资源供应，以保持传输层抖动低于临界失败边界，确保在大规模事件期间语音服务的可靠性。

Abstract: Deploying broadband Mission-Critical Push-To-Talk (MCPTT) services over shared commercial infrastructures introduces resource contention during multi-agency responses in mass-crowd events. This study evaluates cross-carrier interoperability and standard versus prioritized quality of service (QoS) frameworks under real-world saturation constraints. We design an empirical multi-carrier field experiment utilizing twelve identical smartphones deployed across multiple physical sectors inside Texas A&M University's Kyle Field during a football game with 105,000+ attendees. Automated voice calls were monitored using Perceptual Objective Listening Quality Analysis (POLQA), packet delivery metrics, and connection rates. The results reveal that voice path failure is isolated to network infrastructure bottlenecks rather than device hardware limitations. Specifically, we identify a sharp, non-linear network failure model where transport-layer jitter exceeding a critical threshold de-jitter buffer underflows, causing structural audio degradation. Priority-managed channels effectively bypass this congestion. This study helps establish an operational insight for emergency planners to mandate network infrastructure, end-to-end network slicing and dedicated resource provisioning capable of keeping transport-layer jitter below the critical failure boundary.

</details>


### [196] [Vorticity Dissipation Based Routing: A Fluid-Kinetic Framework for Loop-Free Transport in Ultra-Dense Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19630)
*Wen-Yu Dong,Weiwei Jiang,Song Zhao,Rui-Si Han,Qi Bi,Sheng Chen*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于涡度耗散的流体力学路由框架(VDR)，利用Helmholtz-Hodge分解将网络流量解耦为无旋需求驱动分量和有旋环路诱导分量，通过梯度流优化消除超密集无线网络中的路由环路问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超密集无线网络中的离散路由协议受限于信令开销和瞬态路由环路，导致无线资源效率下降；现有标量密度连续体模型缺乏表征拓扑异常的向量几何结构。

Method: 引入流体力学-动力学框架，利用Helmholtz-Hodge分解将宏观流量正交解耦为无旋分量(需求驱动)和涡度分量(环路诱导)；定义网络涡度量化拓扑低效性；将路由优化表述为enstrophy泛函上的梯度流，导出涡度耗散方程作为动力学律；采用Lyapunov稳定性分析证明全局enstrophy单调衰减至无环均衡态。

Result: Lyapunov稳定性分析证明该机制确保全局enstrophy单调衰减趋向渐近无环均衡；数值实验验证VDR抑制转发环路、降低端到端时延、保持稳健的数据包传输，在固定区域密集化条件下呈现近线性扩展，并显式考虑了网格依赖的Poisson求解器成本。

Conclusion: VDR通过涡度耗散机制有效解决了超密集无线网络中的路由环路问题，理论分析与数值仿真均证实其在拓扑优化和资源效率方面的显著优势。

Abstract: Discrete routing protocols in ultra-dense wireless networks are constrained by signaling overhead and transient routing loops that degrade radio-resource efficiency. While continuum modeling provides a scalable alternative, existing scalar density approaches lack the vector geometric structure required to characterize these topological anomalies. This paper introduces a fluid-kinetic framework, vorticity dissipation-based routing (VDR), utilizing the Helmholtz-Hodge decomposition. We demonstrate that the macroscopic traffic flux can be orthogonally decoupled into a demand-driven irrotational component and a loop-induced solenoidal component representing routing vorticity. Building on this insight, we define network vorticity as a macroscopic metric to quantify topological inefficiency. Routing optimization is formulated as a gradient flow on an enstrophy functional, yielding a vorticity dissipation equation as the governing dynamic law. Lyapunov stability analysis proves that this mechanism ensures the monotonic decay of global enstrophy toward an asymptotically loop-free equilibrium. Numerical results validate that VDR suppresses realized forwarding loops, reduces end-to-end delay, maintains robust packet delivery, and exhibits near-linear scaling under fixed-area densification while explicitly accounting for the grid-dependent Poisson-solver cost.

</details>


### [197] [Digital Tides: A Fluid-Dynamic Framework for Flux-Aware Infrastructure Provisioning in UAV Logistics Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19638)
*Wen-Yu Dong,Song Zhao,Rui-Si Han,Qi Bi,Sheng Chen*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文针对高频脉冲式物流无人机群引发的"数字潮汐"现象，提出了一种基于流体力学的时空框架和通量感知的非对称激活策略，通过求解连续性方程表征工作负载流的宏观速度场，实现零延迟追踪移动波前，并在服务可靠性与能耗之间达到帕累托最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基础设施供给策略依赖静态快照分析和局部密度估计，无法捕捉计算工作负载的宏观对流效应，导致反应式资源激活存在固有的滞后性，虽能提升名义效率但在推进波前造成关键任务服务丢失。

Method: 开发流体基时空框架，显式求解连续性方程以表征工作负载流的宏观速度场；提出通量感知非对称激活策略，利用导出的信息通量向量作为需求传播的运动学前兆，将激活和去激活动力学解耦；推导瞬时服务可用性和周期平均能效的闭式表达式；制定服务质量惩罚指标评估严格中断约束下的有效能效。

Result: 理论分析证实通量信号的内在空间相位领先特性，证明所提策略生成主动防护环以补偿服务设置延迟（包括移动边缘计算容器冷启动延迟）；数值结果表明该通量驱动策略实现零延迟追踪移动波前，在服务可靠性与能耗之间达到帕累托最优权衡，在动态物流走廊中优于反应式基线。

Conclusion: 所提流体基时空框架和通量感知非对称激活策略为移动计算网络中的可持续资源供给提供了有效解决方案，克服了传统反应式方法的滞后性缺陷，能够在保证服务质量的前提下显著优化能效表现。

Abstract: The emergence of high-frequency pulsating logistics unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) swarms gives rise to ``Digital Tides'', i.e., complex traffic dynamics that challenge sustainable resource provisioning in mobile computing networks. Conventional infrastructure provisioning strategies, which typically rely on static snapshot-based analysis and localized density estimation, fail to capture the macroscopic advection of computational workloads. As a result, reactive resource activation suffers from inherent hysteresis, yielding nominal efficiency gains at the cost of mission-critical service loss at the advancing wavefront. To address this issue, we develop a fluid-based spatiotemporal framework by explicitly solving the continuity equation to characterize the macroscopic velocity field of the workload flow. Building on this framework, we propose a flux-aware asymmetric activation strategy that leverages the derived information flux vector as a kinematic precursor of demand propagation. Unlike symmetric thresholding, the proposed control logic decouples activation and deactivation dynamics. Theoretical analysis confirms the intrinsic spatial phase-lead of the flux signal and shows that the proposed strategy generates a proactive guard ring to compensate for service setup latency, including delays caused by mobile edge computing container cold-starts. We further derive closed-form expressions for instantaneous service availability and period-average energy efficiency. In addition, we formulate a quality-of-service-penalized metric to evaluate effective energy efficiency under strict outage constraints. Numerical results show that the proposed flux-driven strategy enables zero-latency tracking of the mobile wavefront and achieves a Pareto-optimal trade-off between service reliability and energy consumption, outperforming reactive baselines in dynamic logistics corridors.

</details>


### [198] [RFWM: Physics-Guided World Model for Dynamic Wireless Radiance Field Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19709)
*Zijiu Yang,Qianqian Yang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出RFWM，一种物理引导的射频世界模型，通过两阶段训练策略将多模态物理条件映射为时空射频场，显著提升了动态环境中射频场的生成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 射频辐射场建模对无线网络优化和感知至关重要，但在动态和未知环境中仍然面临挑战。现有基于学习的方法虽然能从稀疏测量中合成射频场，但大多难以泛化到动态和未知环境。

Method: RFWM采用两阶段训练策略与物理引导的先验和约束。第一阶段，将预训练的视觉扩散主干网络适配到射频轨迹，预测未来射频序列，并使用Friis引导先验进行粗衰减指导；第二阶段，通过从头训练ControlNet并微调射频适配主干网络学习物理到射频的映射，同时使用六个物理引导正则化器强制细粒度传播一致性。跨高度头部在一个前向传递中生成查询接收器高度的射频轨迹。

Result: 构建了包含7,715个序列（平均33帧）和115个环境的新基准数据集。实验结果表明，RFWM在分布内和分布外设置下相比最先进方法的MSE分别提升了约7dB和3dB。

Conclusion: RFWM通过物理引导建模有效解决了现有方法在动态和未知环境中泛化能力不足的问题，为射频场的时空生成提供了更好的解决方案。

Abstract: Radio-frequency (RF) radiance-field modeling is essential for wireless network optimization and sensing, yet remains challenging in dynamic and unseen environments. Existing learning-based methods synthesize RF fields from sparse measurements, but most struggle to generalize to dynamic and unseen environments. To address this limitation, we propose RFWM, a physics-guided RF world model that maps multimodal physical conditions like visual dynamics and AP configurations to spatiotemporal RF fields. RFWM adopts a two-stage training strategy with physics-guided priors and constraints. In the first stage, RFWM adapts a pretrained visual diffusion backbone to RF trajectories to predict RF sequences from a few past RF inputs, while conditioning the backbone on a Friis-guided prior for coarse attenuation guidance. In the second stage, RFWM learns the physical-to-RF mapping by training a ControlNet from scratch and fine-tuning the RF-adapted backbone, while six physics-guided regularizers enforce fine-grained propagation consistency. Cross-height heads then jointly generate RF trajectories at queried receiver heights in one forward pass. We construct a new benchmark of 7,715 sequences averaging 33 frames across 115 environments for dynamic RF-field generation. Experimental results show that RFWM improves MSE by approximately 7 dB and 3 dB over the state of the art under in-distribution and out-of-distribution settings, respectively.

</details>


### [199] [Proper Sea Surface Roughness Enhances the Performance of Near-Shore Maritime Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19730)
*Wen-Yu Dong,Shaoshi Yang,Song Zhao,Jinyang Yu,Weiliang Xie,Rui-Si Han,Qi Bi,Sheng Chen*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于随机几何的近岸海上无线通信新分析框架，通过引入海况敏感的反射系数替代理想光滑海面假设，揭示海况依赖性可靠性-容量权衡机制，为动态海上信道提供首个可解析的聚合干扰表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有海上信道分析模型依赖过度简化的传播假设（如完全光滑海面），无法准确反映近岸区域船舶分布密度变化及海浪动态特性，导致性能评估失真，影响海上通信系统可靠性设计与资源优化。

Method: 构建物理驱动分析框架：(1) 采用非齐次泊松点过程建模船舶空间分布以匹配真实港口密度；(2) 基于经典瑞利判据推导新型反射系数，显式关联路径损耗与有效波高；(3) 将粗糙度感知信道模型集成至随机几何框架，推导上行覆盖概率和平均遍历速率的解析表达式。

Result: 发现海况依赖的可靠性-容量权衡现象：粗糙海况抑制相干镜面反射导致破坏性干涉零点减少，提升可靠性导向覆盖；但高SINR概率和平均速率性能下降。跨频段消融实验证实粗糙度效应具有波长依赖性，需按工作频率评估反射系数。实测数据支持粗糙度敏感反射机制，但VHF频段粗糙海况验证仍属模型预测。

Conclusion: 建立首个融合海况动态特性的海上信道分析范式，量化海况对通信性能的差异化影响，为近岸海域通信系统鲁棒性设计提供理论依据。强调频率特异性建模必要性，并指出实测验证方向。

Abstract: Accurate performance analysis for near-shore maritime wireless communication is essential for ensuring robust and reliable operations. However, existing analytical models often rely on oversimplified propagation assumptions, such as a perfectly smooth sea surface, which fail to capture the full dynamics of the maritime channel. In this paper, we develop a physically grounded analytical framework using stochastic geometry that bridges this gap. The spatial distribution of vessels is modeled as a non-homogeneous Poisson point process to reflect realistic near-port densities. We replace the idealized smooth-sea assumption by deriving a novel reflection coefficient from the classical Rayleigh criterion, which explicitly links the path loss to the significant wave height. Integrating this roughness-aware channel model into the stochastic geometry framework, we derive new analytical expressions for the uplink coverage probability and average ergodic rate, providing the first tractable characterization of aggregate interference under such dynamic conditions. The analysis reveals a sea-state-dependent reliability--capacity trade-off: roughness-induced attenuation of the coherent specular reflection can suppress destructive-interference nulls and improve reliability-oriented coverage, while reducing high-SINR and average-rate performance. Available measurements support the underlying roughness-sensitive reflection mechanism, but direct VHF validation under rough sea conditions remains unavailable; the corresponding rough-sea results are therefore interpreted as model-based predictions. A cross-frequency ablation further confirms the wavelength dependence of the roughness effect and shows that the reflection coefficient must be evaluated for the operating frequency.

</details>


### [200] [Using Zone-Disjoint Multi-Path Routing Algorithm for Video Transmission over Ah-Hoc Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20148)
*Ali Asghar Khavasi,Nastooh Taheri Javan*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的多路径路由算法，通过选择区域不相交路径而非节点不相交路径来改善自组织网络中的视频传输性能，克服了CSMA/CA效应导致路径不完全独立的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具有网格拓扑的自组织网络中，虽然寻找多路径路由看似简单，但由于CSMA/CA效应，发现的路径并非完全不相交。现有的节点不相交路径方案无法保证端到端延迟和误码率的独立性，因为中间节点为避免干扰邻居而保持静默，增加了延迟。

Method: 本文提出一种新的多路径路由算法，该算法选择区域不相交路径，并结合合适的视频传输方案，用于自组织网络中的视频多路径传输。

Result: 实验结果表明，使用该新算法配合合适的视频传输方案，相比先前使用的算法能够获得改进的性能结果。

Conclusion: 区域不相交路径策略可以有效克服自组织网络中节点不相交路径的局限性，特别是在视频传输场景下，能够显著改善网络性能表现。

Abstract: Finding multi-path routes in ad hoc networks due to their grid topology seems to be a trivial task, but because of CSMA/CA effects in these networks found paths are not completely disjoint unless an appropriate algorithm have taken into account. If such an algorithm provided and designed carefully it could improve multi-path video transmission over these kinds of networks. By using node-disjoint paths, it is expected that the end-to-end delay and BER in each case should be independent of each other. However, because of natural properties and medium access mechanisms in ad hoc networks the end-to-end delay and also BER between any source and destination depends on the pattern of communication in the neighborhood region. In this case some of the intermediate nodes should be silent to reverence their neighbors and this matter increases the end-to-end delay. To avoid this problem, multi-path routing algorithms can use zone-disjoint paths instead of node-disjoint paths. In this paper we demonstrated a new multi-path routing algorithm that selects zone-disjoint paths that in addition used for video multi-path transmission over ad hoc networks. It is shown that by using this new algorithm along with choosing an appropriate scheme for video transmission over discovered paths, our approach receives enhanced results comparing previously used algorithms.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>
